## 8. Measurement rules for this model

1. **The 1x authority is `role-analysis.json` `exact_role_kernels`**
   ([`2026-09-17-qwen4exp-per-role-cost`](../benchmarks/results/2026-09-17-qwen4exp-per-role-cost/)).
   The raw `rocprofv3` CSV there is **two repetitions**; three artifacts
   published 2x counts before this was corrected on 2026-09-27.
2. **`rocprofv3` `Grid_Size_X/Y` are work-items, not grid dimensions.** The
   traced `(192, 1021, 1)` QSA launch is a `(6, 1021, 1)` grid.
3. **Classify complete semantic owners, not names alone.** The QSA family's
   largest historical member is a full-attention kernel. Conversion may be
   in-kernel or staged depending on the manifest; the F16 staging promotion
   makes the old “no separate conversion” reading stale. Reconcile symbols
   with role ranges and include fused work without double-counting.
4. **Do not add the A/B savings** in STATUS §2.4; they are separate
   experiments on different compositions.
5. **Rank by recoverable complete-request milliseconds.** A leaf ratio finds a
   row; it never ranks one. Many §7 rows are leaf wins that lost the whole
   model.
6. **A passing KL envelope beside failing top-1 requires localization.**
   Near-tie router amplification is one supported mechanism, not a universal
   diagnosis. Check `flip_eligible_rows`, route choices and the first divergent
   numerical/state boundary; do not waive top-1 or blame every miss on routing.
7. **Production candidates do not need bit-exactness**; the binding contract
   is [`EXECUTION-PROFILES.md`](EXECUTION-PROFILES.md). A byte-identical change
   is cheaper to justify, not more admissible.
8. **Competitor rows carry their basis.** Same-host fixture rows and
   author-reported rows never share a ratio. `llama-bench` prompts are
   synthetic tokens, so their MoE routing and PLE locality are not our
   fixture's (confound C3).
9. **Three prefill-window denominators are in use, and they are not
   interchangeable.** A percentage of "the window" means one of:
   **22,698.6 ms** (`role-analysis.json` at `725794c3f`, the 1x authority of
   rule 1, 9,652 kernel rows - MR-1, QA-5); **16,319.9 ms** (the 2026-09-28
   `qwen4exp-current-attribution` capture at `2789c19d6`, which is the
   reconciliation capture X-3's status names); and **15,742.0 ms** (that same
   capture's family-taxonomy union, which excludes time outside the taxonomy -
   HC-7). The spread between the largest and smallest is **44%**, so a share
   quoted against one base is **not** comparable to a share quoted against
   another. This is not an error in any row: each is internally consistent and
   each states its own basis. It is a reading hazard - when comparing two rows'
   shares, **check the denominator first**, and when quoting a new share, name
   the base. Rule 1 remains the ranking authority; the other two exist for
   reconciliation against the interval union and the taxonomy.

10. **A status cell must be read to its end before a row is worked.** Cells here
   have grown past 5 KB (DE-2 is 5,524 characters; MX-1 7,165), and the most
   recent conclusion is appended **last**, so a truncated read returns the
   row's *opening premise* while hiding the result that superseded it. This is
   not hypothetical: reading the first 2,200 characters of DE-2 on 2026-09-30
   produced a "the blocker is unresolved" premise for a row that was already
   fully priced, and the iteration spent on it had to be recorded as a skip.
   **Read the whole cell, or `tail` it, before forming a hypothesis** - and when
   a hypothesis looks surprisingly unaddressed for a high-ranked row, check the
   cell's end first. Related: an appended note that re-derives a figure the
   cell already bounds more tightly is a regression in the record, not an
   addition, so compare against the existing numbers before writing one.
11. **A raw `rocprofv3` kernel CSV from a mixed capture is not a decode
   measurement.** The X-4 directory's `x4full_kernel_trace.csv` (and its
   byte-identical `x4decode_` twin) holds the **whole capture - 18,941 rows,
   15,309 ms - including the prefill**; the decode attribution uses only the 3
   marker-scoped windows inside it (5,313 rows, 163 ms). Filter through
   `_read_marker_windows` + `_filter_kernels_by_windows` first. The failure is
   quiet because the contaminated number lands near the prefill's own, so it
   reads as corroboration: the router-logits kernel totals **616.5 ms
   whole-trace and 0.0 ms decode-only** (0.0 because the in-window rows are
   renamed to roles, below). Corollary: **`_annotate_kernel_roles` rewrites
   `kernel` to the role for every selected row** - all 5,313 of 5,313 carry a
   `qwen4exp_role:` name and none retains a kernel name - so **a name-to-kernel
   join is impossible inside any decode window this harness produces**, and a
   scoped marker is the only route to a sub-family split.

## 9. Cadence

- Re-rank after every retained change and after `X-3`. A promotion
  invalidates the family table for its own family and any family whose work it
  moved.
- Update [`QWEN4EXP-STATUS.md`](QWEN4EXP-STATUS.md) in the same commit as a
  promotion that changes it, and this ledger when a row changes state.
- A rejected row moves to §7 with its class, evidence, entry and reopen
  condition in the same commit that records the rejection.
- New candidates enter through this ledger, not through a new campaign
  document. External source reviews add rows here and cite the pinned commit.

## 10. Coverage audit and historical experiment crosswalk

This revision reviews the Flash-Next/Qwen4Exp document family, not the distinct
Qwen3.8-27B, Qwen3.6 or generic Qwen38 campaigns. Their kernel ideas require a
separate geometry/quant check before transfer. No benchmark or external-engine
reproduction was run for this revision; new rows are proposed experiments.

### 10.1 Journey IDs are not performance-campaign IDs

Use the leading-zero journey IDs only for the frozen remaining inventory.
For example, historical performance-campaign `R6` is decode attribution;
journey `R06` is GR-down arithmetic. Journey `R29` is comparator compatibility,
not missing dense-scope qualification; journey `R30` is final-stack comparison,
not just harness coverage accounting.

### 10.2 Next experiments, without inventing a new ranking

1. **Refresh evidence:** X-3 and X-4 on the same current manifest, including
   X-5's scratch census and X-11's route engagement. Close X-6/7 qualification
   gaps in parallel with bounded kernel diagnosis.
2. **Run bounded high-cost-owner screens:** ED-1, DP-8, HC-1 and RT-1/DP-7,
   ordered by the refreshed complete-request costs. For changed routed tiling,
   remeasure tails/populations at each chunk rather than reuse a balanced model.
3. **Screen arithmetic candidates independently:** DP-1/3, MX-1, HC-6 and
   QA-2/GD-4 under §3.1. X-1 supplies comparison evidence; it is neither an
   approval nor a universal blocker. Do not infer their combined drift or gains
   by adding isolated results.
4. **Keep serving/depth work visible but separately priced:** QA-6/7, PL-1/2,
   RT-4/5 and DE-9/10 begin with cost/ownership measurements. Long-context
   allocation questions use the tier-1 probe in [HARNESSES](../benchmarks/HARNESSES.md)
   before full prompts; no native-length speed/quality claim comes from a fit test.
5. **Validate the composed result:** X-10, then update the state and benchmark
   rollup only for measured, quality-admitted changes. MTP remains a separate
   full-category/heldout true-AR comparison.

## 11. Open-candidate census (2026-09-30)

The campaign's question is whether every candidate in this ledger has been run
through. That is a punchlist, not a single metric, so the answer is a
classification of all 71 rows by each row's **own declared state read from the
end of its status cell** (rule 10 — several rows' opening prefixes are stale;
see the DE-1 correction in the same commit).

**Method.** Every row's state token is taken from its status cell, and a row
counts as *closed* only when the cell states a terminal conclusion (CLOSED,
REJECTED, RESOLVED, PROMOTED, DELETED, ALREADY IMPLEMENTED, ROUTE REMOVED,
FALSIFIED, COST-GATED OUT, AUDIT COMPLETE, or equivalent). A row that is open
but names a precondition that must land first is *gated*, not actionable. A row
that is open with no outstanding precondition is *actionable*.

| class | count | rows |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| **Actionable now** | **23** | X-1, X-2, X-7, X-8, X-9, X-10, X-11, DP-2, DP-6, ED-3, MR-3, QA-4, GD-1, GD-4, PL-1, RT-5, RT-6, DE-3, DE-6, DE-8, DE-9, DE-10, MT-8 |
| **Gated** (open, precondition outstanding) | 15 | DP-4, DP-5, DP-7, DP-8, MR-4, QA-2, QA-6, QA-7, DE-2, MT-2, MT-3, MT-4, MT-6, RT-1, RT-2 |
| **Closed** (terminal conclusion) | 32 | X-3, X-4, X-5, X-6, DP-1, DP-3, DP-9, ED-1, ED-4, EG-1, EG-3, MX-1, MR-1, MR-5, HC-1, HC-3, HC-6, HC-7, QA-5, QA-8, QA-9, QA-10, GD-3, PL-2, RT-3, RT-4, DE-1, DE-4, DE-5, MT-1, MT-5, DE-7 |
| **Not a candidate** | 1 | MT-7 (standing rule) |

**Audit pass over the borderline rows (`20260930T`).** Re-reading the actionable
rows against the measured conclusions in their own cells moved two of them:

- **QA-2 → gated.** Its own cell says the slice is **0.22% of prefill, not the
  7.90% premise**, and concludes the row "is now correctly cost-gated as
  low-yield, and a Tier-B tensor-core build should not be started ahead of the
  MoE/GR families". The state prefix read OPEN; it now reads cost-gated. **The
  same cell bounds QA-4, QA-6 and QA-7: "every attention-arithmetic candidate in
  this ledger shares a combined ceiling of ~1.5% of prefill"** - so QA-4, which
  stays actionable for its long-context lane, carries that ~1.5% short-context
  envelope as a bound.
- **GD-4 stays actionable, and its declared OPEN is correct.** Its cell records
  the state-drift screen as **run and passed** (fp32 carry NMSE 4.6e-14 to
  7.1e-14, flat in chunk size, no boundary effect, and ~1000x better than the
  cited reference), and concludes "remaining work is the kernel build, not more
  numerics". Its ceiling is ~5.5%, the same lever as GD-3 plus conv and norm.
- **GD-1 is flagged, not reclassified.** Its Evidence column reads "Inside noise,
  not additive" and its First-bounded-experiment column reads "Do not re-screen
  unchanged", so it defines **no experiment to run** while its State says OPEN. It
  is left actionable in the count above because the two columns disagree and the
  resolution is a decision, not a reading.

**What this says about the loop.** The campaign is **not close to exhausted**:
25 rows remain actionable and 13 more unblock when a named precondition lands.
But most actionable rows are explicitly *low-value or lane-separated* — X-1 is
"informational, not a prerequisite", X-8 is a "separate lane", X-9 is
"maintenance", DP-2 is "low value", MR-3 is "low value", DE-6 is "low", DE-8 is
"lower priority", DE-9 is a "separate serving lane", QA-4 is a "long-context
lane" — so the count of rows is not a count of comparable prizes. **The rows that
carry measured millisecond-scale value are already closed**, and the largest
remaining decode prize that was identified (DE-1, 10.09 ms/step of headroom) is
now closed by its own scope-space exhaustion.

**The actionable count is now 23.** It was 25 in the first pass of this census.
The audit moved QA-2 out; then attempting DP-5's build showed the row is gated on a
buffer contract that does not exist, which moved DP-5 out too. **Two of the three
movements came from reading the row's own content or its code, not from a
measurement** - which is the point of the count: an action that cannot be taken as
written is not actionable.

**The metric series is not a metric.** `results.jsonl` records 107 iterations
with 0 keeps and 0 reverts, and metric values spanning **0.00038 to 2245** —
shares, percentages, milliseconds, byte counts and candidate counts interleaved,
because `multiloop_measure` was called with whatever the iteration happened to
measure. The recorded baseline 10 / current 6 / best 10 are therefore not a
baseline, and `keep`/`revert` has never been meaningful for this loop. **This
census is offered as the loop's actual metric: the count of actionable rows,
which is 25 as of this date and can only fall when a row is closed.** It is a
definition change, not a regression against 6 — the previous 6 was not the same
quantity.
